124:["$","p",null,{"children":"Every version comes from .devcontainer/tool-versions.env, the repo's declared\nsingle source of truth, sourced the way .github/actions/setup-bazel/action.yml\nsources it (set -a / source / set +a). 1f0:["$","p",null,{"children":["Purpose:\nApply kernel traffic shaping via ",["$","$La8",null,{"termId":"htb","category":"Hardware and safety","short":"Kernel traffic shaping used by download-limit. Often missing on Spark; then the wrap soft-fails.","children":"wondershaper"}]," on the default-route interface.\nSupports fixed Mbps caps and an ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"auto"}]," mode that measures download Mbps\n(duration HTTP probe first, then Ookla, then speedtest-cli) and applies\nfloor(0.85 x measured_download_mbps). Auto measurements are cached 24h on the\nhost (not MODELS_DIR). Apply is verified via tc qdisc (or mocks).\nThe wrap subcommand always clears limits on EXIT/INT/TERM so a killed download\ncannot leave the host permanently throttled.
